# Guanzhi 官制 - 剥夺唐爵效果 (scope: 被剥夺者)
# 爵头衔销毁时, 爵级 trait 与虚封/实封户数 trait 一并消失
guanzhi_clear_tangjue_effect = {
	debug_log = "GUANZHI clear tangjue entered"
	if = {
		limit = {
			primary_title = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_tj }
			any_held_title = { NOT = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_tj } }
		}
		random_held_title = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_tj } }
			save_scope_as = guanzhi_old_title
		}
		set_primary_title_to = scope:guanzhi_old_title
	}
	every_held_title = {
		limit = { has_variable = guanzhi_is_tj }
		destroy_title = this
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_guogong }
		remove_trait = tj_guogong
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_yi_3000 }
		remove_trait = tj_yi_3000
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_jungong }
		remove_trait = tj_jungong
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_yi_2000 }
		remove_trait = tj_yi_2000
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_xiangong }
		remove_trait = tj_xiangong
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_yi_1500 }
		remove_trait = tj_yi_1500
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_xianhou }
		remove_trait = tj_xianhou
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_yi_1000 }
		remove_trait = tj_yi_1000
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_xianbo }
		remove_trait = tj_xianbo
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_yi_700 }
		remove_trait = tj_yi_700
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_xianzi }
		remove_trait = tj_xianzi
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_yi_500 }
		remove_trait = tj_yi_500
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_xiannan }
		remove_trait = tj_xiannan
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_yi_300 }
		remove_trait = tj_yi_300
	}
	guanzhi_tangjue_clear_shifeng_effect = yes
	guanzhi_clear_tangjue_flags_effect = yes
	# 注意: 不清 var:guanzhi_tj_rank —— 册封效果开头先调本效果, 随后还要读该变量选爵级
	guanzhi_fix_titleless_nickname_effect = yes
	debug_log = "GUANZHI clear tangjue done"
}

# 仅清实封 trait (加食实封授新替旧用, 不动爵)
guanzhi_tangjue_clear_shifeng_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_shi_100 }
		remove_trait = tj_shi_100
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_shi_200 }
		remove_trait = tj_shi_200
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_shi_300 }
		remove_trait = tj_shi_300
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_shi_500 }
		remove_trait = tj_shi_500
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_shi_700 }
		remove_trait = tj_shi_700
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = tj_shi_1000 }
		remove_trait = tj_shi_1000
	}
}

		limit = { has_character_flag = guanzhi_tjf_xian_1165 }
		remove_character_flag = guanzhi_tjf_xian_1165
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = guanzhi_tj_xian_1166 }
		remove_character_flag = guanzhi_tj_xian_1166
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = guanzhi_tjf_xian_1166 }
		remove_character_flag = guanzhi_tjf_xian_1166
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = guanzhi_tj_xian_1167 }
		remove_character_flag = guanzhi_tj_xian_1167
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = guanzhi_tjf_xian_1167 }
		remove_character_flag = guanzhi_tjf_xian_1167
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = guanzhi_tjholder }
		remove_character_flag = guanzhi_tjholder
	}
}
